# Ross selected setup to intent contract

The production owner is `RossMomentumStrategyV1.process_watchlist`: canonical registry result, DecisionEngine selection, same-family execution-timeframe TriggerEngine, structural price mapping, existing permission/tradeability gates, then intent/cycle selection. `decision_policy.build_trade_intents` is a separate consumer; passing its tests alone does not prove this production boundary.

The original authorities 06 section 8 and 09 section 9 name ORB/premarket-high, micro pullback, bull flag and consolidation breakout as initial families. Failed breakout is caution/exit, with VWAP context. The 68-item reference vocabulary is not blanket entry activation. Accepted flat-top and three-bar/second-pullback extensions retain their existing session, input and trigger policies. Candle-only, disabled halt and risk-off signals do not authorize long entries.

A selected entry must retain its own numeric trigger and structural stop, rationale and approved target model. Entry completeness is now checked before candidate ranking on the local repair branch described below; the defensive selected-use check remains. The generic optional target field does not override Ross's PR1030 target-presence guard. The production intent now retains `target_model`; this descriptive model is not a fabricated numeric take-profit. A missing target or missing canonical selected contract blocks intent with an explicit terminal reason even if the trigger fired. Target-less detectors remain unavailable for intent until their setup-specific target policy is supplied through the canonical result. This candidate attaches the newly user-authorized native Three-Bar/Second objectives; their unchanged confidence gate still blocks PAPER admission. Native long ORB now uses the newly user-authorized descriptive objective “Continuation above the completed opening-range high.” under its existing detector/trigger gates; see the final ORB section for policy provenance and proof limits.

Core canonical contracts supersede contextual trigger guesses for the same family. PRE/RTH_OPEN use the existing genuine 10s execution stream; steady sessions use the existing 1m stream. Missing/stale execution provenance stays non-actionable. Micro pullback exports its detected pullback high/low; consolidation exports its own range and uses the existing range-break primitive with same-stream volume confirmation. Explicit structural stop and acceptance invalidation remain separate trigger facts; trade geometry uses the structural stop when supplied.

Tests in `test_ross_selected_setup_handoff.py` include real consolidation registry-to-intent checks and explicitly synthetic pre-produced contract handoffs through production functions. They do not certify natural Focus selection, news coverage, Paper order lifecycle or trading readiness. No risk/execution/session/catalyst/volume thresholds are changed.

## Accepted-main setup-family audit — 3 November 2026

Audit source pin `a66b43f2b44d98405d1dc30bc62f8c359a5f3fbc`, tree
`04a58818e178764c28e0fecab472fe1dbbbc0154`. This section preserves the baseline
counterexample and recommendation; the local pre-ranking repair/update below
supersedes its selection-gap status without changing the family target inventory. This updates the existing handoff
audit, not another setup catalogue. The isolated optional-import repair changes
neither strategy, pattern, trigger, management, risk nor execution source.
Original authorities/specifications remain the [strategy contract](../strategy/ROSS_MOMENTUM_STRATEGY_CONTRACT.md),
[PR1029 detection report](../certification/PR1029_REMAINING_ROSS_PATTERN_DETECTION_CERTIFICATION.md),
[PR1030 mapping report](../certification/PR1030_ENTRY_STOP_TARGET_EXIT_MAPPING_CERTIFICATION.md)
and [Bull Flag/Micro relationship policy](ROSS_BULL_FLAG_MICRO_PULLBACK.md).
Old reports describe the layers they actually tested; later accepted source is the
current invocation authority. No registry label is a certification of raw intent.

### Shared real invocation, provenance and downstream boundary

`RossMomentumStrategyV1.process_watchlist` in
`src/strategies/ross_momentum_strategy_v1.py` builds authoritative runtime inputs,
runs Level/Structure/Setup/Trigger engines, then `RossPatternRegistry.run`, then
canonical-result trigger enrichment, `DecisionEngine.compute_decision`, same-family
trigger selection, confirmation, selected-contract/geometry/target checks,
tradeability and per-cycle selection. It does not simply call the standalone
`decision_policy.build_trade_intents` helper. The pre-registry contextual
`SetupEngine` emits PMH, ORB, First/Micro Pullback, Bull Flag, Flat Top, HOD,
VWAP_RECLAIM_CONTINUATION and Consolidation candidates; this is distinct from
calling every canonical detector.

Runtime `patterns/pattern_trace.py::_fetch_runtime_timeframe_bars` requests existing
10s/1m/5m streams. `patterns/pattern_inputs.py::build_authoritative_pattern_inputs` normalizes
original aware bar times, records timeframe/indicator/level provenance, and uses
`policy/pattern_input_policy.py`. PRE/RTH_OPEN primary=1m, execution=10s, context=5m;
RTH_MID/RTH_LATE primary=1m, execution=1m, context=5m. Required/preferred family
checks are applied to the active session plan; a blanket all-day 10s requirement
would misstate the policy. Existing freshness bounds are10s60s/1m180s/5m900s.
Provided indicator values are labelled PRESENT; missing values are computed only
from usable primary candles where supported (EMA9/20, sufficient-history EMA200,
VWAP, MACD), otherwise MISSING/UNAVAILABLE. PRESENT is a provenance label, not an
independent audit of a caller's indicator claim. Optional EMA200/MACD handling
varies by family; no indicator alone creates entry authority.

`setup_fidelity.py::blocking_input_reason` and `apply_detected_setup_fidelity`
enforce scoped BLOCK/non-long/risk-off/missing trigger-stop-rationale rules. The
numeric defaults in `PatternBase._detected` infer trigger/stop from supplied level
context; those are NOT proof of each family's own structural anchors. Runtime
selected-contract review must distinguish them from explicit detector numeric
fields. No target is supplied by that base. `_target_model_from_setup` requires
existing `target_suggestion` or an ABCD projection; it does not manufacture one.
A descriptive target model is not a numeric take-profit/R:R certification.

For eleven families (ORB/PMH/Micro/Bull/Flat Top/Consolidation/Three-bar/Second/Key Level/First Pullback/ABCD),
`_canonical_pattern_trigger_candidates` replaces same-family contextual triggers
with explicit numeric canonical contracts and a genuine execution-stream receipt.
Other registry families do NOT automatically gain this bridge because they have
an isolated trigger evaluator. Contextual setup aliases are normalized separately;
for example Momentum Reclaim normalizes to VWAP_RECLAIM_CONTINUATION, while the
trigger registry has MOMENTUM_RECLAIM. Do not equate those routes without checking
actual emitted setup identity.

After raw formation, `StrategyRunner.process` forwards intents. Core's ACTIVE
manager path in `core/orchestrator.py` preserves arbitration, execution integrity,
position-truth and fresh-quote checks. In READ_ONLY only eligible post-guard intents
receive the existing canonical `build_decision_artifact` ID and actual
`RiskEngine.evaluate_trade_intent`; a real READ_ONLY execution denial is expected.
Five unmatched positions globally block NEW entries AFTER raw formation and BEFORE
canonical artifact/risk admission, so no downstream risk input is expected under
that veto. The locally approved broker-larger correction below also blocks this
shared portfolio entry boundary while same-side broker quantity magnitude exceeds
system quantity, including entries in another symbol; warning classification and
exit/protection ownership remain unchanged. A later unreachable legacy risk block is not a proof of this path.
PAPER/LIVE canonical risk integration is not certified by the bounded READ_ONLY
repair. No runtime/broker operation is part of this audit.

### Bull Flag / Micro Pullback first

`setup_engine/setup_families/bull_flag.py::BullFlagPattern` is the canonical owner:
standalone breakout uses9 candles (3 impulse, up to5 flag, breakout), EMA9>EMA20,
VWAP/trend, flag depth/width/declining volume and breakout-close checks. Its numeric
flag-high/flag-low trigger/stop/invalidation and descriptive measured-move target
are explicit. `evaluate_formation` uses8 closed structural candles and returns
CONTEXT/non-entry. `micro_pullback.py::MicroPullbackPattern.evaluate` requires at
least5 candles, initial impulse,1-3 controlled bars, <=65% retracement, continuation
close above pullback high and EMA9; it exports its own high/low and Prior high/HOD
target. `evaluate_readiness` is an explicit separate armed mode, not confirmation.

Core wires the existing TradeManagementEngine onto the live Ross strategy.
`compose_bull_flag` therefore governs the normal composed Bull Flag route: positive
conId, eligible session, PRESENT streams, closed parent bars, parent security/
session/origin validity, eligible later same-security Micro child, no reused child,
and child stop respecting parent invalidation. Missing parent/child/security is
rejection, never unpaired standalone fallback. Bull trigger delegates to Micro
confirmation at the recorded child event; standalone trigger crossing/wick/spread/
volume controls are a different supported use. Existing consumed-child persistence
and `prepare_relationship_intent` prevent repeated child use. A repeated unchanged
signal is not a new opportunity.

Management profile BULL_FLAG_MICRO_2R_V1 freezes actual reconciled E0/S0/R0 only
after terminal initial fill; the2R once-only floor-half reduction, floor-quarter
adds, positive confirmed quantities, pending reservations, max logical adds,
reconciliation, stop protection and restore rules belong to the existing management/
risk/execution owners. A target description alone does not activate this profile
on another family. Standalone families retain existing generic management controls;
no new partial/R:R policy is inferred from PR1030.

Existing tests: `test_bull_flag_trigger.py` (armed/fired/shape/liquidity),
`test_micro_pullback_execution_refinement.py` (detection, missing levels, armed/fired,
refinement), `test_ross_bullflag_micro_management.py` (real registry+composed child ->
actual strategy -> risk/in-memory execution; missing/stale/cross-security/parent/
child/duplicate/restart/fill/stop boundaries), `test_bull_flag_pipeline_end_to_end.py`
(standalone detector/trigger/decision layer), `test_ross_selected_setup_handoff.py`
(real Micro numeric contract and synthetic selected handoffs).
`test_readonly_manager_risk_handoff.py` replaces the strategy after Core wiring,
uses standalone real BullFlag detection and synthetic supported-news/cached Focus,
and proves actual manager artifact/risk/ownership boundaries. It does NOT by itself
prove the composed relationship/news/Core combination. Integrated PR1159 now joins
canonical news/cache, real Focus and Core-wired composition through raw formation
and the five-position veto (`test_joined_core_bull_micro`). Its original five-position artifact/risk calls
are deliberately forbidden. The local joined-risk extension below now verifies
the intact composed route through active Core canonical artifact creation and
actual READ_ONLY RiskEngine denial using separately complete/reconciled synthetic
position truth; the five-position path remains blocked. Neither layer is natural
market or executable-management certification.

### Every registered enabled family: current coverage map

Scope:28 registry entries (26 TRADE_READY,2 PROBATIONARY),27 unique detector IDs;
Ross also contains6 inactive candle placeholders. The map includes registered
families that are currently blocked; it does not activate them. Every row shares
above session/input/catalyst/quote/ownership/risk gates. Minimum bars below are
local detector minima, not sufficient runtime data requirements.

Source abbreviations: **RF**=`src/setup_engine/setup_families/ross_families.py`;
**PB**=`src/setup_engine/setup_families/pullbacks.py`; **BR**=`src/setup_engine/setup_families/breakouts.py`; **CP**=`src/strategies/common/patterns/`;
**TR**=`src/strategies/common/triggers/trigger_registry.py`. All canonical detectors
are instantiated by `setup_engine/registry.py::build_tradeable_patterns` and invoked
by Ross registry except explicit placeholder skips. **C**=explicit canonical-result
execution bridge above; **X**=only contextual setup route; **G**=no complete actual
Ross trigger production route established. Existing tests are under `tests/`.
**D** means deterministic detection evidence, **H** a tested synthetic handoff;
neither is natural evidence. No natural positive formation is established for ANY row.


Current reconciliation uses the accepted records through PR1186 plus this batch's explicitly labelled pending Micro evidence. Source keys: **SP**=`src/strategies/ross_momentum/strategy_policy.py::POLICY_V2` and its named SetupFamilySpecV2/trigger entries; **S**=`STRATEGY_SPEC_ROSS_CAMERON_MOMENTUM.md`; **R**=`ROSS_MOMENTUM_PATTERN_REFERENCE.md`; **B**=`ROSS_MOMENTUM_PATTERN_DETECTION_BLUEPRINT.md`; **A**=`TRADING_OS_MASTER_CATALOGUE/03_STRATEGIES/P01_ROSS_MOMENTUM/GOVERNANCE/ALGORITHM.md`. **H1–H5** reproduce the user's historical five-tier planning vocabulary and explicitly assign the remaining families: H1 opening repertoire/First/Micro; H2 HOD/Opening Drive/Bull; H3 reclaim/pullback/stair continuation; H4 measured/level/geometric structures; H5 warning/reversal context. HALT is H2-special only as a data-dependent continuation concept and remains disabled. These are coverage/implementation priorities, not a new live rank. Each row also shows the unchanged native V1 exclusivity rank from `src/core/engines/setup_hierarchy.py`; absent families have rank99/no suppressor rule. V1 now qualifies complete own contracts/ready triggers before that exclusivity, then retains DecisionEngine compatibility/confidence/tie ordering. `src/strategies/ross_momentum/hierarchy_policy.py::SESSION_TIER_MAP` remains the separate existing decision_policy route: PRE GAP/PMH first; RTH_OPEN Opening Drive/GAP/PMH before ORB/HOD, then First, then Micro; RTH_MID First/Bull/ABCD before structural families; RTH_LATE stair/VWAP before range, then Micro. Thus historical First/Micro H1 does not silently replace those session exceptions or promote Opening Drive from native entry to generic context. No hierarchy is described as absent. The additional FAILED_ORB_FAKEOUT metadata declaration is warning-only/SHORT-entry-denied; all28 registry names are now explicitly represented in SP, with27 deduplicated detectors. It creates no new route or short permission.

All rows retain existing Ross session, catalyst, liquidity/RVOL, quote, ownership and portfolio gates. SP session/timeframe tags are reported verbatim; required runtime inputs come from `src/strategies/ross_momentum/policy/pattern_input_policy.py::_requirements`, not from treating a PRE/RTH tag as a candle timeframe. Where no dedicated requirement exists, the row says so; this is a policy-coverage gap, not an invented universal input rule. **Q** means only an intrinsically complete contract plus its own canonical occurrence's actual registered trigger can be actionable; equality/volume/forming/clock laws remain family-specific. Without those, detection/armed context is not entry readiness. Invalidation remains each owner's numeric acceptance/structural level; expiry is only existing freshness/parent-event authority, with no new universal timer. **M0** means no recorded standalone family-specific executable recurring-management/final-exit proof here. Existing global risk/protective exits remain authoritative; absence of family evidence is not evidence that all common exit policy is missing. Selected initial protection alone is not recurring management; inspect existing applicable policy before changing code or asking for a new profile. **BM** is only the same-security consumed-child BULL_FLAG_MICRO_2R_V1 relationship profile; its approved whole-share/risk/capital/truth/protection laws remain, never inferred from a descriptive target. Warning/context rows carry no new dispatched exit policy. skipped `inactive_placeholder`; additional heuristics list is empty. Standalone
common candle/context functions must not be counted as enabled Ross entries.

only checks trigger/stop fields IF a supplied fixture detects; it neither proves
positive detection for every family nor checks target or actual V1 handoff.

### Simultaneous families, duplicates and demonstrated selection gap

`DecisionEngine` rejects inactive/non-entry/risk-off candidates, suppresses entries
when parabolic risk is present and rejects opposing-direction conflicts. Compatible
long candidates are ranked deterministically by execution/session compatibility,
contextual setup compatibility, trigger/invalidation availability, non-fallback,
score/confidence and pattern ID. Lower-ranked compatible candidates are explicitly
recorded `dropped_lower_priority_compatible_candidate`; this one-winner behavior
is intentional arbitration, NOT silent deletion or independent confirmation.
It does NOT rank target completeness or trigger readiness. A higher-ranked setup
waiting for its own trigger cannot borrow a different family's ready trigger.

The selected family gets its own trigger; after confirmation the actual V1 code
checks its selected target contract. On failure it continues to the NEXT SYMBOL,
not the next same-symbol family. A controlled counterexample uses an actual Micro
positive detector result (existing Prior high/HOD model), and a synthetic high-score
Three-bar result restoring its real missing target. Micro alone produces one
READ_ONLY raw intent; together, in either input order, Three-bar wins, fires then
fails `missing_target`, and Micro is logged lower-priority and never reconsidered.
This proves a selection/eligibility ORDERING GAP under simultaneous inputs; it
does NOT prove such a combination occurred naturally or invalidate deliberate
one-winner/risk-off/capacity policy. The failed winner and lower loss are visible.

Per-cycle strategy selection ranks only ready candidates by trigger quality,
spread/extension and existing one-submission/capacity limits; explicit terminal
outcomes distinguish lower rank/capacity/no-intent. Relationship child IDs are
consumed/reserved through existing lifecycle authority. GAP_GO/GAP_CONTINUATION
are a duplicate detector invocation (28 objects/27 IDs;34 total registry objects/
33 IDs including placeholders), not two independent opportunities. No loop-level
dedup removes the duplicate evaluation; single selected pattern prevents two
same-symbol strategy outputs in this path. The legacy
`StrategyRunner._apply_one_setup_per_symbol` helper is NOT called by current
`StrategyRunner.process`; do not cite it as the runtime suppression authority.
Core arbitration/integrity/ownership remains a separate post-formation boundary.

### Target gaps, recommendation and evidence limits

NO target model is supplied by the actual ORB, HOD, Opening Drive, Cup Handle,
EMA/VWAP Pullback, Three-bar, Second Pullback, Stair-step, Range, Ascending Triangle
or Pennant detector. Some are blocked earlier by missing actual trigger production.
Existing models for Bull/Micro/Flat Top/Consolidation/First Pullback/PMH/ABCD/GapGo/
KeyLevel/MomentumReclaim/GapFill are not blanket numeric take-profit or lifecycle
certification. Not every model-bearing family has a complete current V1 route.

Highest-priority demonstrated repair recommendation: enforce the ALREADY mandatory
selected-entry target/contract eligibility BEFORE ranking entry candidates, through
the existing selected-setup/DecisionEngine authority; retain explicit missing-target
reasons and one-winner policy, and keep risk-off/conflict signals intact. Do not
add a target, enable a family, choose around ownership/freshness/session/capacity
vetoes, or broaden a trigger bridge as part of that change. Validate a complete
candidate winning over a known target-less one, all-target-less rejecting, no
cross-family target borrowing, opposing/risk-off suppression, deterministic ties
and no change to post-guard admission. This is a recommendation, NOT implemented
or a claim that all nonselected signals should trade. Separate missing family
bridges/structural anchors and policy decisions remain explicitly outstanding.

Natural evidence remains separate: archived successful safety receipts establish

## Local pre-ranking entry-completeness repair — 3 November 2026

Separate branch `fix/ross-entry-contract-selection` starts at reviewed
`89f57a01045d5c158a68eac94c9e9e9258f07b0f` /
`4b7d1402d2aa19cca3a4cc4c97ce86cdc2a4215b`. Dependency repair remains in its

Implementation commit `b6d8c611004055047be2cccd9028bbe9f2e91c97`, tree
`dea9f788a0103e460ae0482aa3915e1a93a4f421`. Local only, not merged/CI/natural proof.

`decision_policy.validate_entry_contract` composes the ALREADY existing canonical
`is_tradeable_entry_candidate`, risk-geometry predicate and target-model resolver
in their existing policy owner. The standalone intent policy and V1's defensive
selected-use check reuse that same composition instead of duplicated rules.
No target, numerical R:R threshold, trigger authority, source or family is added.
The basic detection validator is unchanged: incomplete detection/context remains
visible, not rewritten into a detector failure just to prune the candidate pool.

The actual V1 invokes the existing DecisionEngine with this explicit Ross entry
validator in BOTH existing arbitration calls. Generic engine callers that omit it
retain their previous behavior. Engine preserves original source objects only
inside the bounded current call; no source object is exported/serialized as part
of its result. It first retains inactive/non-entry/risk-off suppression and original
opposing-direction conflict rejection. It then validates intrinsic entry contracts
before compatibility ranking. Every excluded candidate receives the validator's
actual reason in existing `rejected_candidates` plus the structured rejection log.
Ranking key, scores among eligible candidates and one-winner policy are unchanged.
An incomplete warning or opposing candidate is not filtered away to permit entry.

Before: higher-ranked target-less Three-bar won, later failed `missing_target`,
viable Micro was never selected. After: Three-bar is recorded `missing_target`
before ranking; the real Micro positive result wins in BOTH input orders and
retains its own setup ID/family, numeric entry, structural stop, target model and
rationale through actual `process_watchlist` raw intent. Its descriptive target is
not converted into a fabricated numeric take-profit. Synthetic high-score controls
are explicitly labelled, not market opportunities.

All-incomplete inputs use the existing
`CANDIDATE_REJECTED_INSUFFICIENT_QUALITY` / `all_detected_candidates_rejected`
no-entry decision; candidate-specific reasons are retained. V1 emits the existing
decision-stage blocked terminal, not a selected fired-trigger/no-intent success.
Target-less rows in the baseline family map still have NO target policy: they are
now excluded from the entry ranking pool, not enabled or assigned targets.
Context-only setup candidates without their own complete entry fields likewise
reject before ranking, with actual missing-stop/trigger reasons. A late missing
selected contract remains defensively blocked; no canonical contract is created
from a contextual guess.

The selected eligible winner still must pass its own trigger, confirmation,
source/quote/timeframe freshness, session, geometry, tradeability and capacity gates.
No next-family fallback was added after an unconfirmed trigger, risk denial,
submission failure or unknown broker outcome. Core's ownership veto remains after
raw formation and before canonical artifact/real risk admission. Existing actual
Core READ_ONLY handoff/mode/ownership regressions were included in affected checks.

## Exact-candidate review and occurrence binding — 3 November 2026

Verified clean input `9322e0c3fab3f38337040778aa2e9df73f8b4dba`, tree
`a0555f25321c6a4bc53ccd606a0858be53ac2479`. Reviewed arbitration commit
`b6d8c611004055047be2cccd9028bbe9f2e91c97` separately from inherited optional
imports/configuration and baseline audit documentation. Canonical validation is a
composition of existing predicates, reads candidate fields only, and does not
query services, refresh data, evaluate risk or grant a session/ownership override.
Risk-off/context and direction-conflict precedence, ranking key and one-winner
policy were correctly retained. No target was invented. The late defensive
validation and post-selection risk/ownership/execution boundaries remain necessary.

BLOCKER FOUND: selection validated a specific result, but subsequent lookup used
only pattern ID/family. A rejected same-ID occurrence could supply the first trace,
contract or trigger; a later non-detected trace could also overwrite the real
candidate's detected state in normalization. New controls reproduced3failures/
1pass0.57s (missing-target and not-detected occurrences in both orders). A separate
control reproduced confidence contamination when a detector error emits a trace
without a result: positional trace/result zip shifted the next rejected result's
confidence onto the actual selected Micro trace. That failed0.50s after correcting
required fixture constructor fields (the constructor error is not defect evidence).

Minimal fix within existing owners, commit
`9b35e96a5647a5d0094fb927f03da9da2eeab616`, tree
`3d8b3b3d4522a7db61732efeec56c9c791ff902b`:

- Ross-opted-in DecisionEngine preserves original per-call result ordinal and
  returns `selected_candidate_index`; ambiguous same-ID traces cannot override
  canonical result facts. No arbitrary decision IDs or source objects are exported.
  Generic engine callers without the entry validator retain previous output schema.
- Existing canonical trigger candidates carry `canonical_result_index`, copied
  by TriggerEngine. V1 selects the exact chosen occurrence's trigger, contract and
  matching trace, not the first member of a family/ID. Source contract remains the
  owner for its own stop/target/rationale/metadata. Result indices preserve gaps
  from non-detected candidates; they are local list bindings, not persistent trust.
- Trace confidence is assigned through the same identity-bound correspondence,
  not positional zip across possible error-only traces. Invalid/noninteger/bool/
  out-of-range ordinals, wrong identity or ambiguous trace/result correspondence

There IS a precise neighboring-family rule: `src/strategies/common/patterns/pattern_premarket_high_break.py:113-114,135-137` sets buffer=max(0.01,PMH*0.001), stop=PMH-buffer and separate invalidation=PMH. Its `trigger_premarket_high_break.py` requires completed-price acceptance/cross/reclaim semantics, distinct from GAP_GO's existing high-touch evaluator. These are approved PREMARKET_HIGH_BREAK implementation facts, NOT prior GAP_GO approval. Adopting them for GAP_GO requires an explicit policy extension. Existing order-router `_resolved_tick_size/_round_price_in_favor` defaults/clamps/rounds for execution; it is not authority to infer a missing tick or silently change a strategy stop. No such behavior is changed here.

**ONE concrete proposal, NOT IMPLEMENTED — PMH-only regular-open GAP_GO:**
1. Scope only GAP_GO/PMH_BREAK during the already configured normal RTH_OPEN phase, with all current catalyst/selection/session/freshness/quote/ownership requirements. HOD_BREAK, ORH/BREAK_AND_HOLD, generic continuation and opening-pullback stops stay entry-blocked; no fallback among variants.
2. Structural reference window is the same security and New York trading date's premarket interval [04:00,09:30). PMH is the maximum high of authoritative complete data covering that interval; source bars must be fully completed within it. This exact window is part of the new proposal, not a claim that current GAP_GO proves full-session coverage. Boundary/value/provenance become fixed at09:30 before any RTH confirmation. Use the existing reference/market-data/input owners; a latest50-bar maximum, stale previous-day value, unexplained field or caller “verified” flag cannot replace full-window authority. Current input projections do not alone certify that authority; missing coverage/provenance remains blocked. No new acquisition or budget extension is authorized by this proposal.
3. Entry confirmation is the first eligible completed10s upward close-cross: previous completed10s close<=fixedPMH, current completed10s close>fixedPMH (coherent high>=close). Both bars must be genuine, fresh and from the same observation context. A high-only touch or close==PMH is insufficient; post-break holding/reclaim variations are not included. Freeze source reference before those bars and exclude confirming/future bars from the reference window. This narrows GAP_GO's current touch semantics prospectively; it is not an existing policy claim.
4. Structural risk stop S=PMH-max($0.01,0.001*PMH), explicitly adopting the neighboring PMH buffer formula. No new offset, extra buffer or price rounding. Require authoritative security-specific tick/increment evidence valid for S and entry; compute/compare with decimal price arithmetic. If the exact S is not on a known permitted grid, or tick authority is absent/conflicting, reject rather than round/default to$0.01. No generic stop, opening-window minimum or alternate anchor.
5. Setup invalidation is separate at PMH: completed10s close<=PMH invalidates acceptance. An intrabar dip below PMH but above S is not, by itself, close-based invalidation. The structural risk barrier is intrabar price<=S once entry/protection is relevant. Before entry, a confirming bar that also reaches/breaches S is rejected because OHLC cannot establish break/stop ordering. No claim of certified live stop execution or tick ordering follows. Existing attempt/cooldown and protection owners remain mandatory; no re-anchoring, budget renewal or post-risk/execution fallback.
6. Absent/ambiguous/stale structure, date/security/window mismatch, incomplete coverage, unknown tick, malformed bars, unconfirmed cross or missing source/clock binding all reject with explicit reasons. Fixed PMH cannot be refreshed on failure to manufacture a new eligible opportunity.
7. Existing risk sizing/limits consume the actual entry reference and S; `RiskEngine.evaluate_trade_intent` at risk_engine.py:952 enforces required hard stops and :1002-1010 computes per-share risk=abs(entry-S) and profile-size total risk against existing limits. Do not size from PMH invalidation instead of S, promise approval or change caps. No new numeric target/management policy. READ_ONLY still forbids execution; the five unmatched positions veto new-entry admission after raw formation before artifact/risk.

**Specific decision required:** approve or reject this PMH-only policy extension (fixed premarket window, completed10s upward close-cross, existing PMH buffer formula with no rounding/default tick, separate close-based invalidation/intrabar stop). No policy rule has been activated. Approval would require a subsequent bounded implementation in existing GAP_GO/input/V1/special-dispatch/contract owners and honest validation of reference/tick availability. In particular, the current candidate's generic GAP_GO invalidation<reference guard intentionally remains; allowing invalidation==PMH for this proposed variant would itself be part of that later approved change. Other-family guard semantics must not be relaxed.

Local recommendation: binding fixes are reviewable; keep production GAP_GO entry blocked and withhold any production-intent/readiness claim until a policy decision and required input authority exist. Corporate/SEC/listing remain PARKED; GUI draft, historical receipts and all five-position protections preserved. No provider/broker calls, observation, push, merge or new policy implementation occurred.

### GAP_GO stop-authority reconciliation — documentation only, 4 November 2026

Preserved candidate **`dfeb857955a61b610ac70d3f63bc3f71c71855b0`**, tree **`ee3c19e7a24b35dbda5f590c691aa44b47b68cba`**, clean `fix/gap-go-structural-handoff`. Existing review/binding failures, 48-control passing receipt, prior overlapping251/44/2 receipts and active-manager five-position receipt remain intact. No suite rerun for documentation.

**Correction/clarification of the preceding proposal:** the PMH-buffer formula belongs to PREMARKET_HIGH_BREAK, not GAP_GO authority. The proposed fixed04:00–09:30 full-session proof, RTH-only scope and mandatory completed10s upward close-cross were additional proposed restrictions, not recovered Ross requirements. They remain unapproved and are not prerequisites that the user must accept to resolve a stop. In particular, existing strategy_policy.py:678 explicitly permits intrabar10SEC Gap&Go entries; requiring a completed trigger candle would narrow that policy. Completed structural anchors must not be confused with a mandatory execution-candle close. The current blocked candidate's completed-execution plumbing is a conservative test path, not certification of the approved intrabar variant, and must be reconciled before any production activation.

Directly retained educational originals supporting a more precise GAP_GO stop were not located in this bounded relevant-material check. Linked external materials were not fetched. Root specifications and the training report are retained summaries; project policy/source code and prior family repairs are separately identified above. Legacy `src/strategy/gap_and_go_strategy.py` is a translation plugin without a numeric initial-stop contract, not the actual Ross V1 authority or a reason to revive a legacy route.

#### Variant reconciliation and already-defined parameters

- **Direct PMH/HOD/open-range breakout:** existing policy permits immediate/intrabar10SEC execution with contextual gates. Actual special evaluator implements PMH/HOD high-or-close>=selected reference and ORH current+prior-close hold. These exact comparisons are project implementation facts, not claimed verbatim Ross teaching. Reference priority/kind/value/occurrence are now explicit and reviewed. Numeric GAP_GO structural stop, buffer/tick treatment and separate acceptance invalidation remain undefined; no pullback exists automatically.
- **Opening First Pullback / later Micro Pullback after drive:** structure and execution are separate. Existing canonical First/Micro detectors define their own measured selected windows, triggers and lows. For those qualified canonical entries, there is no need to invent a new GAP_GO candle window or duplicate stop detector. Keeping their own family IDs/contracts under the prevailing market context preserves truthful ownership and one-winner arbitration. A macro GapGo label does not confer permission to borrow their stops when a different occurrence wins.
- **Retest/hold of a broken level:** source summaries describe it as a separate micro/retest form; “just under” remains nonnumeric unless the actual selected canonical retest/pullback/PMH contract supplies its own rule. It cannot be merged with a high-touch direct entry by implication.
- Setup invalidation versus risk stop remain different meanings even when they share a numeric low. Existing TriggerEngine uses close<=invalidation as its acceptance rejection; existing risk/protection owners consume the hard-stop reference. Generic wording “loss of structure” alone does not establish whether a newly enabled GAP_GO variant should fail intrabar or on close. Current generic-code behavior is not evidence of a chosen family-specific stop policy, and OHLC cannot prove intrabar order.

#### Only decisions that remain genuinely missing

1. **Do direct GAP_GO variants need standalone entry support now?** Concrete alternatives: keep them blocked and allow naturally qualified existing First/Micro entries to retain their own contracts (no new stop rule); or explicitly approve one direct variant with a complete stop/acceptance rule. No new decision is required to continue already-supported families.
2. **If enabling a direct variant, choose its exact numeric hard-stop authority.** Alternatives: a specifically approved below-selected-level buffer/tick rule; or require an actually qualified existing pullback contract and trade that family instead. The neighboring PMH formula is an available proposal with its original scope disclosed, not an existing GapGo default. No arbitrary offset, N-bar window, LOD/indicator substitute, unverified tick fallback or rounding is selected here.
3. **For that newly supported variant only, define acceptance invalidation separately:** loss of selected breakout level versus loss of structural low; intrabar failure versus completed-close failure, with exact equality and same-bar break/stop ambiguity handling. Existing pullback family rules need not be reopened. Existing intrabar execution permission is already established; a mandatory close-based entry or RTH-only restriction would be another deliberate change, not necessary stop-gap resolution.
4. If requesting an additional **GAP_GO-branded opening-pullback path** rather than existing canonical First/Micro identities, explicit composition/identity ownership would be needed; do not invent another window/detector or competing signal. This is optional, not on the critical path, and no new subsystem is proposed.

Reference freshness/session/security provenance, unchanged risk sizing/hard-stop requirements, read-only execution denial and five-position post-formation/pre-artifact/risk veto are existing requirements, not user choices to relax.

**Publication assessment:** the reference/binding/explicit-block changes can be published for independent exact-head review and required CI while all unsupported GAP_GO entries remain blocked. Publish as fail-closed contract/handoff preparation, not production GAP_GO intent completion. Must retain unmodified-detector zero-intent tests, explicit block reason and honest synthetic-stop labels. The completed-execution test restriction and lack of usable stop/source authority must be stated; before any variant is enabled, align timing with the already approved intrabar policy or obtain explicit narrower-policy approval. No claim that buffered PMH policy, full-session window, protective execution or natural progression is implemented/certified. Publication itself remains subject to separate authorization; no push/merge here.

**Recommendation:** do not approve a universal GAP_GO stop or bundle the earlier new restrictions. Keep direct GapGo entries blocked; retain naturally qualifying First/Micro entries with their existing measured stops/identities. Only decide a direct variant's standalone stop/invalidation rule if standalone direct-entry support is required. This avoids inventing a duplicate pullback strategy merely to clear a label.

No source/test edits, provider/broker calls, observation, new policy, push or merge. Candidate, receipts, historical UNKNOWNs, GUI draft, five-position protections and PARKED corporate/SEC/listing work preserved.

Meaning of classifications: APPLIED means a specific bounded historical effect is established by committed owner evidence and its reviewed write ordering, not that every effect completed or exactly-once recovery is certified. UNAPPLIED requires an explicit bound owner rejection before mutation; missing rows, current zero values, broker-flat or terminal status are not negative application proof. Everything else is UNKNOWN. Test-controlled live state/call ordering is a separate oracle used to expose ambiguity; it is never promoted to durable operational proof. Audit timestamps/UUIDs and current projections alone cannot establish ordering/completeness/idempotency for a different update.

Existing owners/evidence inspected: ExecutionEngine.collect_relationship_updates/_record_fill_and_position and process-local position_records; Core._apply_execution_results_to_trade_management; TradeManagementEngine._save_relationship_state/restore_relationship_state/on_relationship_order_update (persisted request, fills/notional, exact seen_exec_ids, consumption, protection refs and progress/rejection records); CapitalManagementAuthority and StrategyCapitalAllocationAuthority conversion/reservation/release; PostFillLifecycleEngine creation/record_exit_fill/stop audit; StorageEngine and SQLiteStore. Committed evidence was read through independent read-only SQLite connections, not the writer's buffers. Ten active-Core fault stages retain independent synthetic Filled1@10.91/status/complete conId-bound broker position1; competing callbacks/order snapshots retained separately. No successful internal result, prepared permission or owner state is injected. Restores create new actual owner instances; two guarded fresh Python subprocesses read SQLite read-only and prove the progress guard still blocks before a callback read. No StartupRecoveryAuthority.run, adoption, reconstruction, clearing or replay invoked.

Demonstrated wiring gap: Core passes StorageEngine to both capital/allocation owners. Those _audit methods return early when insert_capital_audit_event/insert_strategy_allocation_audit_event is absent. Those methods exist on SQLiteStore but NOT on the passed StorageEngine facade. Revised controls assert the actual owner/facade contract and preserve UNKNOWN. Simulated audit-boundary failures are labelled as such, not actual failed SQLite writes through a nonexistent interface.

ONE PROPOSED CONTRACT — proof-only, owner-scoped resolution; not yet approved/implemented:
1. Existing ExecutionEngine coordinates assessment of ONE retained update with exact mode/account/security/relationship/decision/client/native/execution identity, ordered cumulative transition and original clocks. Independent complete execution/order/position/protection facts must be newer than uncertainty and mutually consistent. Status/flat/current values alone never prove historical application; unsupported corrections, missing completeness or conflicting identity are UNKNOWN.
2. Each existing owner attests only its own effect. Capital/allocation need stable effect identity, committed conversion/release/reservation disposition and before/after owner-state receipt; lifecycle/protection need bound validated application result and authoritative working/cancel/terminal order evidence; TME needs bound cumulative/consumption/parent-child receipt; registry needs its own committed projection receipt. Persistence adapter supplies committed reads/transaction evidence, not semantic permission. Positive receipts prove bounded effects, not whole-update completion. UNAPPLIED needs a specific pre-mutation rejection or approved durable no-application protocol; missing completion never qualifies.
3. Resolve without replay ONLY when all required effects are individually proven applied exactly as intended, their committed post-states reconcile with complete independent facts, no contradictory fill/unknown reservation/protection work remains, and every associated pause/failure cause is identified. A future owner-issued resolution receipt must bind exact original evidence/version and clearing scope in the SAME existing records, retaining original failure history. It may retire that update's progress blockage only; cannot reset child/milestone consumption or release unrelated reservations, and cannot clear other recovery causes.
4. Current lifecycle rejection/over-exit and all UNKNOWN effects remain quarantined under this contract. No completing an unapplied effect, rebuilding ledger, adopting a position, corrective trade, accounting replay or automatic clearing is authorized. Original rejection evidence remains immutable even if a separately authorized future reconciliation eventually resolves it. Ordinary terminal/absence/stop-cleanup proofs retain only their existing scope. A boolean plan.paused or global failed state cannot safely be cleared for one cause unless provenance establishes ALL its causes; current booleans alone do not supply that evidence.
5. Smallest prospective implementation, only after approval: expose the already-existing audit APIs through StorageEngine, extend existing owner/audit/management records with stable per-effect identity, durable post-state/application outcome and cause-scoped resolution provenance. Couple receipts to committed owner-state semantics; mere forwarding, UUID audit rows or a success flag is insufficient. Use existing SQLite/state owners, no parallel journal/engine. Do not retrofit historical APPLIED from newly available APIs. Add a read-only assessor first; no replay/clearing route while UNKNOWN remains. Durable projection/rebuild semantics would require their own explicit policy if receipts cannot prove completion.

Specific decision needed: approve this PROOF-ONLY/NO-REPLAY resolution policy and prospective owner-bound evidence/cause tracking, with UNKNOWN/contradictory cases staying blocked; or separately define who may authorize ledger reconstruction for irreducibly ambiguous historical effects. Existing documents approve neither reconstruction nor generic clearing of these records. This assessment stops here: no production API forwarding, new journal, resolver, clearing, unpause, replay, adoption or corrective order implemented. Existing family inventory counts unchanged/overlapping; no family/management/broker readiness upgraded. One-share/five-position protections/GUI/historical receipts preserved, corporate/SEC parked, direct GAP_GO blocked. Broader exposure reconciliation/requested quantity separate and open. Integration adds no production changes beyond reviewed candidate. Accepted scope ONLY prospective CONVERT/RELEASE preparation and owner-observed outcomes. atomic_application=false; inspector resolution remains blocked. No historical upgrade, replay, clearing or unpause authority.

Bounded next DESIGN recommendation (not implementation): specify a single prospective CapitalManagementAuthority.CONVERT owner transaction, using StorageEngine/SQLiteStore's existing database and audit facility. Current durable state includes lifecycle audit/prepared/outcome rows and separately persisted relationship/recovery records; capital/allocation exposure dictionaries and reservation objects, plus execution position_records, are process-local. CapitalManagementAuthority.convert_reservation_to_exposure mutates symbol/strategy used exposure and selected reservation before finish_owner_effect; StrategyCapitalAllocationAuthority does its independent mutation/receipt. SQLiteStore.insert_owner_effect_record commits only an INSERT. None is an atomic financial-effect transaction today.

Minimum genuine boundary would require moving the selected capital owner's authoritative reservation disposition and affected symbol/strategy exposure projection to versioned durable owner state in that existing database; this must replace process-local authority for that enrolled path, NOT create a second authoritative balance ledger. Compute/validate a proposed delta without mutation, then one owned SQLite transaction checks exact identity/expected state version, persists all affected owner post-state and immutable effect/outcome evidence together, and commits. Existing callers cannot nest unrelated transactions. Update in-memory views only after commit; a post-commit crash leaves durable owner state authoritative. Same-key conflicting facts reject; an unknown commit response requires read-only identity/state verification, never financial replay. This is a prospective state-ownership migration requiring separate approval; not merely forwarding an audit method or enclosing Python mutations in BEGIN/COMMIT. It must not initialize new authority by reconstructing ambiguous historical balances.

Only that owner's durable projection and receipt can share this proposed transaction. Allocation conversion/release, lifecycle/position accounting, terminal reservation cleanup, relationship/action consumption, protection and external orders remain separate until explicitly included by their owners. No claim that one capital transaction resolves a whole execution update. Pre-commit rollback/crash leaves no committed effect; post-commit/pre-memory-refresh requires read-only reload of authoritative state, not reapplication. Broker event receipt before local persistence, cross-owner commit gaps, external order/protection races and receipt loss/corruption remain quarantine conditions. Independent complete identity-bound executions, cumulative quantity/price, terminal/open-order and coherent position/protection facts must still reconcile with committed local effect identities and state versions. Net-flat or terminal status alone proves no historical local application.

Required design/control scope: exact owner-state schema and migration/initialization eligibility, SQLite durability/connection/transaction ownership, concurrent compare-and-set, reservation lifecycle compatibility, process restart as read-only restoration, and fault injection around every commit/publication boundary. Risks: dual authority during migration, floats/units/rounding drift, differing capital/allocation lifetimes, older snapshots/schema readers, and nested transaction/locking changes. Preserve established arithmetic/tolerances; do not authorize new accounting from absent records. Whole-update safe clearing requires a larger cross-owner/cause-scoped recovery design. Keep it blocked until all relevant effects have durable proof and independent reconciliation; unrelated startup/ownership/protection/recovery causes remain. No such design implemented here.

One-share/five-position protections and uncertain-action ownership retained; inventory counts unchanged and overlapping. Broker atomicity, broader exposure reconciliation and requested-quantity reporting remain open. Corporate/SEC parked; direct GAP_GO blocked. No provider/broker calls, runtime, observation, trading or corrective holdings action. PAPER_READY=NO; PAPER_READINESS_GATE=FAIL.


### Atomic capital-owner boundary — bounded design-only assessment, 6 November 2026

Verified origin/main68f5c22b7337b7f76915aebb0dfbb89ad72236d0/treebbeada563d167a83c07728a107759c1aa30c7345. Isolated docs/atomic-capital-owner-design descends from integration receipt ea85d191 and acceptance464c72b4; production/workflows match accepted main. Applicable ledger read; no AGENTS.md found in workspace. No experiment needed: source establishes the missing boundary. No tests or CI run, no implementation or publication. Historical UNKNOWN and quarantine remain unchanged.

Owner/read-write inventory: src/core/capital_management_authority.py owns _reservations, _used_exposure_by_symbol, _used_exposure_by_strategy and startup-only _recovered_trade_ids (118-121). Reads are active_reservations,total_reserved_capital,total_used_exposure,symbol_exposure,_reserved_symbol_notional,_find_reservation; evaluate_entry combines owner exposure/reservations with externally supplied portfolio context using existing max/plus rules (185-189). active_reservations currently exposes live mutable reservation objects. Writers are reserve457,attach_order502,release_reservation507,convert_reservation_to_exposure549,release_exposure617,recover_from_lifecycle661 and recover_from_open_orders693. No other production module directly accesses those private dictionaries in repository search. ExecutionEngine is the production caller: construction110, _recover_capital_state212-239, _capital_admission_check406, attach_order1343, exit release1734, reservation release2216 and conversion2251. Allocation owns different similarly named dictionaries; do not conflate them. _audit937 depends on an absent facade method and is not financial authority. Existing SQLite capital_audit_events and position_lifecycle_transitions are audit, not restorable capital state.

1. Smallest complete scope and files

Migrate ONE capital authority per stable private account/provider-environment/mode scope, covering all of its reservation and exposure writers together. Do not enroll only CONVERT/RELEASE or only Bull/Micro: that would miss admission reservations and other users of portfolio capital. Scope is stable across runs (never run_id/client71/symbol alone); require explicit unambiguous existing account identity from established configuration/external identity boundary. Do not export it in diagnostics. Missing scope cannot initialize or alias another account. This identity binding is an implementation prerequisite, not invented production configuration in this assessment.

Within existing SQLiteStore add narrowly scoped normalized owner-state tables: capital_owner_state(scope,version,status,schema), capital_owner_reservations(scope,reservation_id,all CapitalReservation fields) with unique scope/decision identity, and capital_owner_exposure(scope,dimension,key,amount) for existing symbol/strategy projections. These REPLACE process-local authority for enrolled scopes; audit rows stay evidence, not a second balance ledger. No independent event replay framework. Keep exact current float arithmetic/units, integer quantity representation, statuses and release-clamping semantics; broader excess-exit/exposure issues stay separate. Persist terminal reservation records too, so absence is not mistaken for nonapplication. Account/equity/buying-power snapshots and policy/config are admission inputs, not invented balances; bind their version/as-of and decision identity to the admission operation where available, reject missing required facts under existing gates.

CapitalManagementAuthority: all public reads use one consistent durable snapshot; return detached immutable/copy reservation views, never mutable authority. evaluate_entry(reserve=True) computes current limits and reservation under one owner transaction. reserve(decision) must revalidate decision/input/state version under that transaction, not trust a previously approved mutable CapitalDecision; stale approval rejects without routing. evaluate_entry(reserve=False) remains a nonbinding quote. attach_order is a versioned identity-bound mutation; never overwrite a conflicting order. Release reservation (all matching records in one operation), convert and release exposure update owner rows and evidence atomically. Existing recovery methods become validation/read-only loading for enrolled scopes; they must not clear/rebuild durable balances from lifecycle/open orders. Their legacy test behavior can remain only in explicitly isolated non-enrolled simulation.

StorageEngine supplies typed narrow operation/snapshot facade; SQLiteStore owns exact transaction, expected-version checks, bound receipt insertion and rollback. ExecutionEngine changes limited to stable scope/operation binding, startup durable load instead of rebuild, and handling blocked/unknown commit results before dispatch. Existing strategy-allocation, lifecycle and management owners not migrated. Existing capital audit facility/owner-effect table reused for receipts via a non-autocommitting private INSERT inside the SAME transaction; current insert_owner_effect_record cannot be nested because it rejects unowned transactions and commits independently. Existing insert_capital_audit_event uses INSERT OR IGNORE and optional per-write commit, neither sufficient for authority conflict detection. SQLite connection/BEGIN primitives suffice, current public write APIs do not. Add only this bounded operation API, not a generic unit-of-work framework.

2. Transaction, identity and restart invariants

Validate supported schema, scope eligibility, storage durability settings and input/binding before mutation. Require SQLite synchronous=FULL or stronger with supported local durable filesystem/journal settings verified; existing foreign_keys-only setup does not establish power-loss guarantees. Hardware/filesystem honesty remains a limitation. Reject active unrelated transactions; do not commit/rollback another owner's work. One transaction-local connection on its owning thread, bounded busy/lock wait respecting existing deadline; no broker/network work inside transaction. BEGIN IMMEDIATE serializes writers before reading state, recomputing admission and updating. A scope version increments once per committed operation; conditional expected-version validation prevents stale/ABA writes. Independent connections/processes use the same protocol; all writers must be migrated or blocked for enrolled scope. No cached approval bypass.

Stable operation identities: reserve keyed by scope+decision (bound intent/request and approved quantity/notional); attach by scope+decision+client/native order binding; reservation release by reservation+terminal/cancellation/absence evidence identity; CONVERT/RELEASE exposure reuse bound execution/order/decision/relationship/type/cumulative identity, full delta quantity/price/security facts and pre/post state versions. No random fresh operation ID on retry. Exact committed duplicate returns read-only ALREADY_COMMITTED with stored outcome, never mutates or reroutes; differing payload under same logical key is conflict/quarantine. Identity collision/change, cumulative regression, unsupported version or missing independent required terminal/absence authority blocks. Distinguish receipt inspection from permission to retry an entire execution update.

Transaction writes every affected reservation/exposure row plus immutable outcome receipt (including exact before/after version and sufficient post-state) then commits once. Pre-mutation rejected operations may record explicit rejection atomically with no state change. PREPARED is evidence of intention only, never application. No externally visible success/decision.reserved_capital publication before commit. Current audit rows cannot be relabelled atomic. New versioned capital-only receipts may assert atomicity only after this boundary is actually implemented/tested; PR1167 records remain non-atomic forever.

Crash before commit, including after SQL state update but before receipt/commit: rollback exposes neither committed effect nor outcome. Crash after commit before return or memory refresh: reopen read-only by operation identity, load committed owner state; never apply the delta again. Commit I/O error/unknown response blocks until durable state/receipt/version can be read and validated; absent/unreadable/conflicting outcome is UNKNOWN, not permission to retry. Memory refresh failure cannot fall back to old values: invalidate projection, block dependent reads/admission, reload authoritative snapshot. Readers load scope+reservations+exposures in one SQLite read transaction/version, not separate mixed-version property queries. Restart restores only committed enrolled capital state, preserving outstanding uncertain reservations; checks scope/schema/invariants and required independent reconciliation before new entry. Restoring capital state is not clearing startup/pause/cached-update/protection latches.

3. Migration and rollback constraints

Use an explicit supported schema successor to current8; preflight future/unknown DB versions BEFORE initialize_schema can modify anything. Schema migration is transactional, additive to existing database, backed up with a consistent SQLite backup while operational writers are stopped. It creates no authoritative zero balances for historical scopes. Existing audit rows, absent records, current memory, terminal status or broker-flat snapshots cannot seed authority. Mark existing scopes UNENROLLED/QUARANTINED, retain history. No automatic startup enrollment or reset. Only a genuinely new isolated simulation scope can start empty in tests; real deployment needs separately approved evidence/prerequisites for a prospective clean boundary. Until that enrollment contract exists, operational activation is deferred, including for the user's real holdings.

Storage-unavailable/read-only/JSONL backend/unsupported schema in enrolled PAPER/LIVE must block capital admission and retain uncertain reservations/recovery; never switch to memory. This does not authorize disabling independent owned exits/protection: they keep existing safeguards, while failed capital accounting latches recovery. READ_ONLY performs no financial mutation; consistent durable reads must not grant permission. Deterministic storage-free SIM/component tests retain explicit ephemeral authority in their isolated mode; they make no restart/atomic claim. Native provider must not obtain ephemeral fallback by a caller flag. Future tests need both backends' policy parity with new durable restart assertions.

After any durable enrollment, old binaries must be fenced from that scope/database; additive tables alone do not stop old writers ignoring them. Deployment must verify version gates across startup/readers and exclusively switch binaries/DB, not dual-run. Downgrade is not dropping tables, restoring a stale backup or replaying history; stop operational writers and keep scope quarantined until a separately reviewed forward-compatible path exists. Exact schema8 checks in PR1167 validate/inspect paths must be updated deliberately for new schema while still rejecting future versions and preserving old receipt meaning. Financial representation changes, general reconstruction and migration of ambiguous existing holdings are outside this scope.

4. Focused future acceptance (not executed)

Inject crash/failure before BEGIN, after reservation insert/update, after each symbol/strategy exposure write, before/after receipt INSERT, during commit, after commit/before response, and before/during memory refresh. Reopen independent connection/process: only all-state-plus-receipt or neither; ambiguous commit blocks. Cover create/attach/partial conversion/full conversion/reservation release/exposure release and all selected reservation fields, exact duplicate/conflict/cumulative reorder, restart with uncertainty, receipt/schema corruption and missing rows. Two writers concurrently at separating cap must not both reserve; stale quote/reserve and mutable returned-object tests cannot bypass transaction. Reader snapshot cannot mix versions. Lock timeout/disk-full/read-only/unsupported storage/persistence failure cannot publish permission or silently lose reservations. Test schema8 migration refusal to backfill, future-version rejection before writes, old-writer fencing, isolated SIM parity, READ_ONLY no mutation, and normal PAPER ownership/UNKNOWN/broker-larger/five-position controls. Historical identical-evidence counterexample stays negative. All external calls hard-fail; no accounting replay, latch clearing or whole-update completion.

5. Recommendation and Ross impact

REVISE the earlier conversion-only proposal to this complete single-capital-owner reservation lifecycle before implementation; defer operational enrollment until stable scope, clean-boundary authority and old-writer fencing are concretely approved. It is bounded to existing capital/storage/execution files but is a real state-authority migration, not a one-line receipt fix. No need for a parallel ledger. A staged offline implementation could prove local capital atomicity in fresh synthetic scopes, not resolve the user's historical interrupted records.

Explicitly OUTSIDE transaction: strategy allocation, lifecycle/accounting, position registry, child consumption/relationship plan persistence, protection and broker order/fill effects. Their crash windows and independent execution/order/position/protection reconciliation remain. A successful capital transaction cannot clear their recovery blockers or certify whole-update recovery. Ross gains a future durable proof for one local financial owner; whole-update cause-scoped recovery, independent snapshot verification, remaining allocation/lifecycle boundaries, broker atomicity, natural progression and execution readiness still require separate work/policy. Existing family inventory counts remain unchanged/overlapping. Historical UNKNOWN, five-position/one-share protections, broader exposure and requested-quantity issues preserved. Corporate/SEC parked; direct GAP_GO blocked. PAPER_READY=NO; PAPER_READINESS_GATE=FAIL.

Reservation state and progress bindings/schema/finite values checked; changed/missing receipt/state integrity rejects. No arbitrary replay from receipts or reconstruction from absent records.

Separate durable pending processing marker precedes financial transaction; only confirmed uninterrupted normal completion retires its own marker. Financial state+receipt commit together; failure before commit preserves prior balances, after commit preserves new balances. Fresh process loads committed state without reapplication. Interrupted/pending records block future mutations, persist across restart and mark existing Execution recovery failure before lifecycle/allocation rebuild or provider inventory. Enrolled startup is read-only load/return; it never adopts positions or reconstructs another owner. No marker resolver/clear/unpause API added. Local errors latch owner failure; observed commit/completion response errors attempt durable quarantine, without overwriting another pending cause.

Explicit remaining boundary: if normal-completion marker retirement commits, its response is lost, AND recording the observed failure also fails, there may be no durable failure-observation marker. ### Full Ross setup-family completion programme — user direction, 8 November 2026

The user expands the task from one-family handoffs to completion of ALL Ross setup families in the existing multi-strategy system. One consolidated programme instruction should authorize research/specification, existing-owner implementation, meaningful validation, independent review, CI and controlled merges across dependency-ordered batches without repeated user copy/paste or routine approval. Current reconciled scope at main fef52a0a: 28 configured registry names, 27 distinct detector identities (GAP_CONTINUATION explicitly aliases GAP_GO), 23 potential long-entry families, plus risk/reversal/special-data roles. All configured names: GAP_GO, ORB/OPENING_RANGE_BREAKOUT, FIRST_PULLBACK, MICRO_PULLBACK, BULL_FLAG, KEY_LEVEL_BREAK, ABCD, CUP_HANDLE, MOMENTUM_RECLAIM, PREMARKET_HIGH_BREAK, HALT_RESUME, PARABOLIC_EXHAUSTION, GAP_FILL, GAP_CONTINUATION, OPENING_DRIVE, OPENING_FAKEOUT, CONSOLIDATION_BREAKOUT, FLAT_TOP_BREAKOUT, ASCENDING_TRIANGLE, PENNANT, RANGE_BREAK, HOD_BREAK, EMA_PULLBACK, VWAP_PULLBACK, THREE_BAR_PULLBACK, TREND_CONTINUATION_STAIR_STEP, SECOND_PULLBACK, FAILED_ORB_FAKEOUT. Do not count the 6 inactive candle placeholders as additional working entry families. The broader vocabulary is evidence to reconcile, not permission to invent extra Ross entry families or silently omit required distinct variants.

Owner map: src/setup_engine/registry.py is the canonical family/alias registry; src/setup_engine/setup_families/ hosts registered implementations/adapters; src/strategies/common/patterns/ and common/triggers/ host reusable structure/trigger functions. Ross strategy_policy.py, hierarchy_policy.py, decision_policy.py and active V1 integration own Ross selection, sessions, eligibility and relationships. Existing Core/risk/execution/lifecycle owners retain order/position/management authority. Shared modules currently depend in places on Ross types/policy: inspect and reduce demonstrated coupling with a minimal compatible interface, not a parallel framework or wholesale migration. Other strategies may reuse shared evidence/detectors and choose their own policies; they must not inherit Ross thresholds, hierarchy or management implicitly.

The older five-tier draft expresses priority and role: Tier1 GAP_GO/ORB/FIRST_PULLBACK/MICRO_PULLBACK/PREMARKET_HIGH_BREAK; Tier2 HOD_BREAK/OPENING_DRIVE/BULL_FLAG; Tier3 continuation/reclaim families; Tier4 structural families; Tier5 reversal/exhaustion non-entry evidence. Reconcile complete membership and session-aware current policy explicitly. hierarchy_policy.py already has SESSION_TIER_MAP, and build_tradeable_patterns already coalesces the declared GAP alias; do not repeat old claims that hierarchy or deduplication is absent. Trace active arbitration, canonical IDs and actual use. Eligibility/own trigger readiness must precede winner selection, risk-off warnings stay authoritative, and an unready higher-tier structure must not starve a valid ready candidate. No blanket session overrides or duplicate orders from overlapping same-symbol patterns. Bull/Micro parent-child composition remains distinct from independent competing setups.

For each family maintain one row in these SAME records: canonical name/aliases/role; exact source references; shared detector and trigger paths; Ross policy/bridge; source and execution timeframes, original-clock/session rules; positive geometry and invalidation; volume/liquidity/trend conditions; measured entry/stop/invalidation/objective; eligibility/hierarchy; entry/re-entry/add/management/exit role where actually authorized; prior proof and remaining gap. Separate original Ross-source statements, newly chosen project engineering/calibration rules and still unsupported assumptions. Generic nonempty target strings do not by themselves establish a usable target/management model.

Completion ladder for entry families: own genuine geometry -> separate armed/ready/invalidated trigger -> occurrence-bound V1 selected intent -> full-registry news/cache/Focus/Core/actual READ_ONLY risk proof -> synthetic PAPER acknowledgement/later broker-event facts/Core accounting/reconciliation/initial stop proof. Then audit and complete already-authorized recurring management through existing owners, explicitly distinguishing standalone from Bull/Micro relationship policies. Do not transplant its 2R profile to other families or treat stop seeding as complete management. For warning/exit-only families prove the correct suppression/management evidence path instead of manufacturing long entries. HALT_RESUME requires authoritative halt/resumption data; inferred gaps cannot certify it. GAP_GO stop/invalidation and Momentum/GAP_FILL generic anchors require real structural authority, not a field-completeness waiver.

RANGE_BREAK, ASCENDING_TRIANGLE and PENNANT currently reuse simple-continuation heuristics: implement and test their own measured geometry before canonical enablement. Research the source corpus for all families; retain sound existing implementations instead of rebuilding them. Centralize measured inputs once per symbol/observation/timeframe with immutable/provenance-safe reuse; independent family evaluations, freshness and invalidation must survive. Keep detection deterministic and side-effect free, avoid network I/O in detectors, benchmark representative full-registry cycles and memory against baseline. No speculative cache, dynamic plugin system, parallel execution, or duplicate management subsystem merely to claim optimization.

Deliver in coherent dependency batches (several related families where appropriate), with one research/inventory pass, shared cross-family contract tests, focused changed-owner checks and required final CI per candidate, no repeated unchanged passing-suite/review/documentation-only loops. Continue automatically between completed batches while execution resources permit; checkpoint completed/next work so resumption uses the same instruction. Preserve exact review/test/merge provenance and historical failures. Never claim all-family or broker readiness while substantive gaps remain; report real external-data dependencies and remaining policy decisions together, without blocking independent work or inventing authority. No real broker/provider activity, trading, holdings/capital mutation, enrollment/resumption or recovery clearing. All broker-truth/five-position protections, parked corporate/listing/SEC and direct GAP_GO block remain until their own existing approved gates are satisfied by demonstrated work; no blanket bypass.
